{"forms":[{"form":"Lots of Fortune","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The primary Arabic lot (or 'part') still in use. It is defined as λ_H ± (λ_☾ − λ_☉) – that is, a longitudinal distance along the horizon from the ascendant, H, that is equal to the longitudinal separation of the Sun and Moon. For some, λ_H + (λ_☾ − λ_☉) is the Lot of Fortune and λ_H − (λ_☾ − λ_☉) the Lot of Spirit; more often, λ_H + (λ_☾ − λ_☉) is the Lot of Fortune during the day and λ_H − (λ_☾ − λ_☉) the Lot of Fortune at night."],"topics":["astrology","human-sciences","mysticism","philosophy","sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"Part of Fortune"},{"word":"🝴"}],"upstream_line":8856815,"upstream_offset":21421601885,"upstream_record_sha256":"4174353148b5f8a61e01d63a090788c653f4513082c22f9e4bd1d57f057593c7","word":"Lot of Fortune"}

{"forms":[{"form":"primitive elements","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An element that generates a simple extension."],"qualifier":"field theory","topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An element that generates the multiplicative group of a given Galois field (finite field)."],"qualifier":"field theory","raw_tags":["of a finite field"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Given a modulus n, a number g such that every number coprime to n is congruent (modulo n) to some power of g; equivalently, a generator of the multiplicative field of integers modulo n."],"topics":["mathematics","number-theory","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An element that is not a positive integer multiple of another element of the lattice."],"qualifier":"lattice theory","raw_tags":["of a lattice"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An element x ∈ C such that μ(x) = x ⊗ g + g ⊗ x, where μ is the comultiplication and g is an element that maps to the multiplicative identity 1 of the base field under the counit (in particular, if C is a bialgebra, g = 1)."],"raw_tags":["of a coalgebra over an element g"],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An element of a free generating set of a given free group."],"raw_tags":["of a free group"],"topics":["group-theory","mathematics","sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"topics":["number-theory","mathematics","sciences"],"word":"primitive root"},{"sense":"element that generates a field extension","word":"generating element"},{"sense":"element that generates the multiplicative group of a finite field","word":"primitive root of unity"}],"upstream_line":7384301,"upstream_offset":16567565711,"upstream_record_sha256":"c2bc754845264145fd17aca0ef7d46d80e818ea3ec2f7d0b495d75b1b29245f4","word":"primitive element"}

{"forms":[{"form":"more technically","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most technically","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Technically, he was Canadian, but everyone assumed he was American.","type":"example"},{"text":"Technically, a guinea pig is not a pig, nor is it from Guinea.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Based on precise facts, which, however, may be contrary to common belief or casual terminology."],"synonyms":[{"word":"stricto sensu"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"technically correct","type":"example"},{"text":"Due to an omission in the peace treaty of the Russo-Japanese War, Montenegro was technically at war with Japan for 102 years.","type":"example"},{"text":"Stalin was not technically the Head of State.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Based on formal (de jure) arguments, which, however, may be misleading."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Although she is technically gifted, her piano playing lacks passion.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In terms of technique."],"synonyms":[{"word":"techniquewise"}]},{"glosses":["In a technical (involving specialized knowledge) way."]},{"examples":[{"text":"For now, it is technically impossible to have a manned flight to Mercury.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["According to the current state of technology."],"synonyms":[{"word":"technologically"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɛk.nɪˌkəl.i/"},{"ipa":"/ˈtɛk.nɪˌkli/"},{"ipa":"/ˈtɛk.nəˌkli/"}],"upstream_line":300266,"upstream_offset":1499106721,"upstream_record_sha256":"86048a113148c622c94fa3182ecb042047bfc1bd5d9c35e456f6c51fb830cc40","word":"technically"}
